===== FILE: tests/test_unit_tools.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from src.components.tool_ui import _get_initials, format_options_display
from src.tools.base import BaseTool, DataType, ToolInfo, ToolOption, ToolResult
from src.tools.colocation.ellipse import EllipseColocationTool
from src.tools.colocation.point import PointColocationTool
from src.tools.cotravel.ellipse import EllipseCotravelTool
from src.tools.cotravel.point import PointCotravelTool
from src.tools.dwell.ellipse import EllipseDwellTool
from src.tools.dwell.point import PointDwellTool
from src.tools.hotspot.ellipse import EllipseHotspotTool
from src.tools.hotspot.point import PointHotspotTool
from src.tools.track.ellipse import EllipseTrackTool
from src.tools.track.point import PointTrackTool


@pytest.mark.unit
class TestDataType:
    """Test the DataType enum."""

    def test_point_value(self):
        """DataType.POINT should have value 'point'."""
        assert DataType.POINT.value == "point"

    def test_ellipse_value(self):
        """DataType.ELLIPSE should have value 'ellipse'."""
        assert DataType.ELLIPSE.value == "ellipse"

    def test_enum_members(self):
        """DataType should have exactly 2 members."""
        assert len(list(DataType)) == 2


@pytest.mark.unit
class TestToolOption:
    """Test the ToolOption dataclass."""

    def test_creation_minimal(self):
        """ToolOption can be created with minimal required fields."""
        opt = ToolOption(
            name="test_option",
            label="Test Option",
            type="int",
            default=42,
        )
        assert opt.name == "test_option"
        assert opt.label == "Test Option"
        assert opt.type == "int"
        assert opt.default == 42
        assert opt.description == ""
        assert opt.min_value is None
        assert opt.max_value is None
        assert opt.step is None
        assert opt.choices is None

    def test_creation_with_all_fields(self):
        """ToolOption can be created with all fields."""
        opt = ToolOption(
            name="count",
            label="Count",
            type="int",
            default=10,
            description="Number of items",
            min_value=1,
            max_value=100,
            step=5,
            choices=[{"label": "A", "value": 1}],
        )
        assert opt.name == "count"
        assert opt.label == "Count"
        assert opt.type == "int"
        assert opt.default == 10
        assert opt.description == "Number of items"
        assert opt.min_value == 1
        assert opt.max_value == 100
        assert opt.step == 5
        assert opt.choices == [{"label": "A", "value": 1}]

    def test_frozen(self):
        """ToolOption should be frozen (immutable)."""
        opt = ToolOption(name="test", label="Test", type="str", default="value")
        with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
            opt.name = "new_name"  # type: ignore[misc]


@pytest.mark.unit
class TestToolInfo:
    """Test the ToolInfo dataclass."""

    def test_creation_minimal(self):
        """ToolInfo can be created with minimal required fields."""
        info = ToolInfo(
            id="test-tool",
            family="test",
            name="Test Tool",
            description="A test tool",
            icon="test-icon",
            data_type=DataType.POINT,
            tab_label="Test Results",
        )
        assert info.id == "test-tool"
        assert info.family == "test"
        assert info.name == "Test Tool"
        assert info.description == "A test tool"
        assert info.icon == "test-icon"
        assert info.data_type == DataType.POINT
        assert info.tab_label == "Test Results"
        assert info.options == []

    def test_creation_with_options(self):
        """ToolInfo can be created with options."""
        opt1 = ToolOption(name="opt1", label="Option 1", type="int", default=1)
        opt2 = ToolOption(name="opt2", label="Option 2", type="str", default="test")
        info = ToolInfo(
            id="test-tool",
            family="test",
            name="Test Tool",
            description="A test tool",
            icon="test-icon",
            data_type=DataType.ELLIPSE,
            tab_label="Test Results",
            options=[opt1, opt2],
        )
        assert len(info.options) == 2
        assert info.options[0].name == "opt1"
        assert info.options[1].name == "opt2"

    def test_frozen(self):
        """ToolInfo should be frozen (immutable)."""
        info = ToolInfo(
            id="test-tool",
            family="test",
            name="Test Tool",
            description="A test tool",
            icon="test-icon",
            data_type=DataType.POINT,
            tab_label="Test Results",
        )
        with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
            info.id = "new-id"  # type: ignore[misc]


@pytest.mark.unit
class TestToolResult:
    """Test the ToolResult dataclass."""

    def test_creation_minimal(self):
        """ToolResult can be created with minimal required fields."""
        result = ToolResult(success=True)
        assert result.success is True
        assert result.data is None
        assert result.geojson is None
        assert result.error is None
        assert result.metadata == {}

    def test_creation_with_all_fields(self):
        """ToolResult can be created with all fields."""
        geojson = {"type": "FeatureCollection", "features": []}
        metadata = {"count": 10}
        result = ToolResult(
            success=False,
            data={"result": "value"},
            geojson=geojson,
            error="Something went wrong",
            metadata=metadata,
        )
        assert result.success is False
        assert result.data == {"result": "value"}
        assert result.geojson == geojson
        assert result.error == "Something went wrong"
        assert result.metadata == metadata

    def test_mutable(self):
        """ToolResult should be mutable (not frozen)."""
        result = ToolResult(success=True)
        result.success = False
        result.error = "Updated error"
        assert result.success is False
        assert result.error == "Updated error"


@pytest.mark.unit
class TestBaseTool:
    """Test the BaseTool abstract base class."""

    def test_validate_options_merges_defaults(self):
        """validate_options should merge defaults with provided options."""
        tool = PointDwellTool()
        options = tool.validate_options({"min_duration_minutes": 60})
        assert options["min_duration_minutes"] == 60
        assert options["max_radius_meters"] == 100.0  # default

    def test_validate_options_uses_all_defaults(self):
        """validate_options should use all defaults when no options provided."""
        tool = PointDwellTool()
        options = tool.validate_options({})
        assert options["min_duration_minutes"] == 30
        assert options["max_radius_meters"] == 100.0

    def test_validate_options_preserves_extra_keys(self):
        """validate_options should preserve extra keys not in defaults."""
        tool = PointDwellTool()
        options = tool.validate_options({"min_duration_minutes": 60, "extra": "value"})
        assert options["extra"] == "value"

    def test_to_geojson_returns_result_geojson(self):
        """to_geojson should return result.geojson when present."""
        tool = PointDwellTool()
        geojson = {"type": "FeatureCollection", "features": [{"type": "Feature"}]}
        result = ToolResult(success=True, geojson=geojson)
        output = tool.to_geojson(result)
        assert output == geojson

    def test_to_geojson_returns_empty_collection_when_none(self):
        """to_geojson should return empty FeatureCollection when result.geojson is None."""
        tool = PointDwellTool()
        result = ToolResult(success=True, geojson=None)
        output = tool.to_geojson(result)
        assert output == {"type": "FeatureCollection", "features": []}


@pytest.mark.unit
class TestToolInstances:
    """Test that all 10 tool instances can be instantiated and have correct metadata."""

    @pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
    def _setup_tools(self):
        """Create all 10 tool instances."""
        self.tools = [
            PointDwellTool(),
            EllipseDwellTool(),
            PointHotspotTool(),
            EllipseHotspotTool(),
            PointCotravelTool(),
            EllipseCotravelTool(),
            PointColocationTool(),
            EllipseColocationTool(),
            PointTrackTool(),
            EllipseTrackTool(),
        ]

    def test_all_tools_instantiate(self):
        """All 10 tool classes should instantiate successfully."""
        assert len(self.tools) == 10
        for tool in self.tools:
            assert isinstance(tool, BaseTool)

    def test_all_tool_ids_are_unique(self):
        """All tool IDs should be unique."""
        ids = [tool.info.id for tool in self.tools]
        assert len(ids) == len(set(ids)), f"Duplicate IDs found: {ids}"

    def test_point_tools_have_point_data_type(self):
        """Point tools should have DataType.POINT."""
        point_tools = [
            PointDwellTool(),
            PointHotspotTool(),
            PointCotravelTool(),
            PointColocationTool(),
            PointTrackTool(),
        ]
        for tool in point_tools:
            assert tool.info.data_type == DataType.POINT

    def test_ellipse_tools_have_ellipse_data_type(self):
        """Ellipse tools should have DataType.ELLIPSE."""
        ellipse_tools = [
            EllipseDwellTool(),
            EllipseHotspotTool(),
            EllipseCotravelTool(),
            EllipseColocationTool(),
            EllipseTrackTool(),
        ]
        for tool in ellipse_tools:
            assert tool.info.data_type == DataType.ELLIPSE

    def test_all_tool_families_are_correct(self):
        """All tools should belong to one of the 5 families."""
        expected_families = {"dwell", "hotspot", "cotravel", "colocation", "track"}
        families = {tool.info.family for tool in self.tools}
        assert families == expected_families

    def test_five_families_total(self):
        """There should be exactly 5 tool families."""
        families = {tool.info.family for tool in self.tools}
        assert len(families) == 5

    def test_tool_tab_labels_shared_within_families(self):
        """Tools within the same family should share the same tab_label."""
        # Dwell family
        assert PointDwellTool().info.tab_label == EllipseDwellTool().info.tab_label
        assert PointDwellTool().info.tab_label == "Dwell Results"

        # Hotspot family
        assert PointHotspotTool().info.tab_label == EllipseHotspotTool().info.tab_label
        assert PointHotspotTool().info.tab_label == "Hotspot Results"

        # Cotravel family
        assert PointCotravelTool().info.tab_label == EllipseCotravelTool().info.tab_label
        assert PointCotravelTool().info.tab_label == "Cotravel Results"

        # Colocation family
        assert PointColocationTool().info.tab_label == EllipseColocationTool().info.tab_label
        assert PointColocationTool().info.tab_label == "Colocation Results"

        # Track family
        assert PointTrackTool().info.tab_label == EllipseTrackTool().info.tab_label
        assert PointTrackTool().info.tab_label == "Track Results"

@pytest.mark.unit
class TestFormatOptionsDisplay:
    """Test the format_options_display helper from tool_ui."""

    def test_empty_dict_returns_default_settings(self):
        """An empty options dict should return 'Default settings'."""
        assert format_options_display({}) == "Default settings"

    def test_single_option(self):
        """A single option should be formatted as 'Label: value'."""
        result = format_options_display({"min_duration_minutes": 30})
        assert result == "Min Duration Minutes: 30"

    def test_multiple_options_joined_with_pipe(self):
        """Multiple options should be joined with ' | '."""
        result = format_options_display(
            {"min_duration_minutes": 30, "max_radius_meters": 100}
        )
        assert result == "Min Duration Minutes: 30 | Max Radius Meters: 100"

    def test_string_value(self):
        """String values should be rendered as-is."""
        result = format_options_display({"algorithm": "dbscan"})
        assert result == "Algorithm: dbscan"

    def test_boolean_value(self):
        """Boolean values should be rendered as True/False."""
        result = format_options_display({"include_outliers": True})
        assert result == "Include Outliers: True"

    def test_float_value(self):
        """Float values should be rendered normally."""
        result = format_options_display({"threshold": 0.75})
        assert result == "Threshold: 0.75"


@pytest.mark.unit
class TestGetInitials:
    """Test the _get_initials helper from tool_ui."""

    def test_single_word(self):
        """A single word should produce one initial."""
        assert _get_initials("Hotspot") == "H"

    def test_two_words(self):
        """Two words should produce two initials."""
        assert _get_initials("Point Dwell") == "PD"

    def test_parenthesized_word(self):
        """Parentheses should be stripped before extracting initials."""
        assert _get_initials("Dwell (Point)") == "DP"

    def test_long_name_caps_at_two(self):
        """Names with more than two words should still return at most 2 initials."""
        assert _get_initials("Very Long Tool Name") == "VL"

    def test_three_words(self):
        """Three-word name should be capped at 2 initials."""
        result = _get_initials("Hot Spot Finder")
        assert result == "HS"
        assert len(result) <= 2

    def test_parenthesized_multiple_words(self):
        """Parentheses with multiple words should strip parens and cap at 2."""
        result = _get_initials("Track (Ellipse Data)")
        assert result == "TE"

    def test_lowercase_input(self):
        """Lowercase input should still produce uppercase initials."""
        assert _get_initials("cotravel analysis") == "CA"
